It's more like... some days I feel almost too upbeat. Like I have so much energy I could fix up the whole house and reorganize the warehouse at work all before dinner. But then, on the flip side, if my husband questions one of my plans or gets in the way, I just... I snap at him over something really small. So it's a mix of feeling really high and then getting really irritable.\n\nDoctor: How many days in a row has this period of feeling unusually high, energetic, or irritable lasted so far?\n\nPatient: It's been going on for about a month now. So, yeah, it's been a solid month of feeling this way every day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period like this in your life before, where you felt unusually high, energetic, or irritable for several days or longer?\n\nPatient: No, I really haven't. No, this is definitely the first time I've ever felt quite like this.\n\nDoctor: During this past month when you have been feeling so energetic and wound up, have you noticed that you need much less sleep than you usually do to feel rested?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, my sleep hasn't really changed. I still go to bed around the same time and wake up when I usually do. I haven't noticed needing less sleep to feel rested or anything like that. I'm just... really energized during the day.\n\nDoctor: During this past month, have you felt like you were unusually talented, powerful, or had special abilities that others don't have?\n\nPatient: No, definitely not. I don't feel like I have any special powers or anything like that. I'm just... you know, a regular person. I just have a lot more energy and feel kind of wound up, but I don't think I'm unusually talented or better than anyone else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any new physical symptoms or health concerns, such as changes in your appetite, weight, body temperature, or heart rate, since this period of high energy started?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed any of that. My heart isn't racing or anything, and I haven't felt unusually hot or cold. I really haven't had any new physical issues come up at all.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
